**UX RECOVERY (branch improve-UIUX, 2026-11-19, Claude Opus 5.5, owner brief 'BrandPilot failed the demo quality standard').** Product-wide simplification, one batch per commit. Inspection (MD Labs on a scratch DB, sample-data CSVs imported as test data, no AI key): (1) Guide panel auto-opens over content on every screen; (2) five places answer 'what next' and the menu has 10 items (Insights / Performance / Improvement overlap); (3) Company knowledge ~7,500px: 8 stacked question cards, all empty topics listed; (4) audit cards carry ~7 blocks each; (5) Settings says data is in browser local storage (false — server DB); (6) long instructional copy (page picker, Home explainer cards); (7) weekly trend shows the unfinished current week as a drop; (8) refresh during a website read loses the read. Batches: B1 shell — guide closed by default, menu 10→8 (Performance = Results · Insights · Leads; AI Director = Ask · Tasks & weekly review), Settings storage copy. B2 Company knowledge + onboarding copy. B3 Insights cards + Home. B4 Studio / Series / Review journeys. Status per batch is recorded below this line.

- **P4 DONE (2026-11-20, branch feat/p4-fact-approvals from improve-UIUX 6dce193; NOT on improve-UIUX; no PR yet).** Server: table fact_approvals (company_id, fact_id, status approved|rejected, by_user, at, review_by, legacy) + company_settings.approvals_since + campaigns.fact_ids (ADDED_COLUMNS). knowledge.listFacts(companyId, status, {now}) gives the effective status approved | expired (review_by passed) | rejected | pending and approval {by email, at, reviewBy, legacy} when recorded; approvedFacts = approved only (so the AI context, audit and QA drop expired and unrecorded facts). knowledge.decide(companyId, userId, decisions, {now}): POST /api/companies/:id/facts/decisions [{id, status approved|rejected|pending, reviewBy?}] — approving needs evidence, review date default +365 days (max 3 years), errors not_found | no_evidence | invalid_review. Tracking starts at the company's FIRST recorded decision: facts approved in the browser state then (and not in that request) become legacy "approved before tracking began"; afterwards an approval only in the browser state is pending; a rejection in the browser state always counts; re-sending an approval without a review date never replaces legacy or a valid approval. GET /facts adds tracked:true once tracked. campaigns mirror factIds; campaignQa check facts-current (linked facts all approved). Page: f.dec markers (approveF a, kReject r, kUnreject p, kReapprove ra); decSync after each save (GET /facts, POST only person-made decisions, re-read; DEC.facts memory); K() uses the server status once tracked (srvOk); apprLine in fact details (recorded by/on/review by — local date; legacy; Review due + Re-approve for 12 months; unrecorded); expired badge "Review due"; Home Needs you lists facts past their review date; campFacts records factIds at generation and strategy approval; factGate blocks downloads (stratOk includes it) with the reason and "Open Company knowledge"; Check & download shows "Facts used: N". Migration: test/fixtures/pre-p4.db (written by scripts/make-pre-p4-db.mjs with the pre-P4 code), test/migration-p4.test.js (columns added, every row kept, 44 browser approvals keep counting, first decision → 44 legacy, report and campaign still read, app sign-in and re-save). No local data/ database exists on this machine. Tests: test/factApprovals.test.js (9), test/migration-p4.test.js (4), ui-tests/fact-approvals.ui.mjs (EN+AR). npm test 400/400, npm run test:ui 11/11. - **P3 checkpoint 1 DONE (2026-11-20, branch feat/p3-number-check; no PR).** server/numcheck.js: extractNumbers (Arabic-Indic digits ٠-٩ ۰-۹, ٫ ٬ ٪, %, "percent / في المئة", thousands, k; dates, years, list markers and plain counts 0-10 not checked), buildPool(db, id, {range, question}) = KPIs of the period + previous period (marked) + changes, row counts, per-channel comparison (+ previous), audit evidence/calc/titles/confidence in EN and AR, approved facts, the question, the period length; each known number carries subject (mobile, desktop, channels, pages, sources…) and metric read from its own clause. checkText: a number matches when the known value rounded to the written precision equals it (after "about/حوالي" a round number may be rounded further); statuses verified (+source) / unverified / other_period (a previous-period number not presented as such) / mismatch (value of another subject or metric = swapped). checkAnswer: main claim = the AI claim marked main (else the first sentence with a number); any non-verified number in it blocks the answer (reason {code, raw, actual}). Director prompt (PROMPT_VERSION director-2026-11-20b) asks for claims [{text, numbers, support, main}]; ask() returns them cleaned. Routes: POST /director/ask adds check (trail detail.check); POST /director/rules = the rules engine on the server (server/rulesDirector.js, same intents and titles as the page version + "what should we do first?" + project experience), checked and recorded with producedBy: 'server'; POST /trail/answer only for the owner's local endpoint (rules → 400); POST /numcheck {texts, range, source:'import'} for pasted answers (trail number_check); POST /numcheck/demo (404 unless BRANDPILOT_DEMO=1) = demoSample (real numbers with deliberate mistakes: wrong rounding, swapped desktop/mobile, previous period, invented, Arabic-Indic invented, fake percentage + true controls) checked by the real check, trail number_check demo:true. Campaign copy: campaignQa check 'numbers' (every number in the copy must be in an approved fact; advisory, does not block downloads). Page: the old page rules engine (director + its project wrapper) removed; numbers render as links to their source (modal: type, wording, period, open its screen) or grey "unverified" with the reason; a blocked answer shows "Held back" with the reason; Import Claude answer checked on the server; Check & download row "Every number in the copy is an approved fact"; demo button "Test the number check" under the Director grounding line (demo mode only). Real AI: claude-sonnet-5-5 with the new prompt, 6 answers recorded in test/fixtures/p3-ai-answers.json (first run had 4 false alarms, fixed: "of"-tied subjects, Facebook+Instagram combined = social); final run 11/11 EN and 8/8 AR verified; no secret found anywhere. Tests: test/numcheck.test.js (13), campaignQa (+1), trail (rules route), ui-tests/numcheck.ui.mjs (2, EN+AR). npm test 434/434, npm run test:ui 17/17. Screenshots docs/ui-audit/screenshots-p3/. NEXT: checkpoint 2 only after owner approval.

- **P6 DONE (2026-11-20, branch feat/p6-track-record from feat/p5-audit-trail d76ccda; no PR yet).** server/track.js + server/trackSchema.js (table track_steps, created by initSchema). When a recommendation becomes a campaign (recCamp) or a task (recTask), the page calls POST /api/companies/:id/track {recId, kind campaign|task, ref, range, windowDays 7|14|28|56|90, default 28}; the server reads the recommendation from its own audit and saves: metric + scope (PLAN per recommendation id, e.g. ch-leak → enquiry_rate on that channel, mobile-gap → enquiry_rate on mobile, seo-q → ctr, src-lowq → qualified_share on that source, cadence → posts, eng-drop → engagement_rate; information gaps get no metric), baseline (the window's length of days ending the day before the start, or at the last data day if earlier), direction, target (baseline moved by MIN_CHANGE 5%), window [start, start+days-1], titles EN+AR, who; idempotent per (kind, ref); trail row step_tracked. The audit keeps the recommendation's subject as a non-enumerable property (auditMeta.decorate) so the plan can read it while the output and baselines stay unchanged. Evaluation (GET /track and after every state save): once the dataset's last date reaches the window end, the step is judged once — met / no change / not met (±5%) / inconclusive (sample below MEASURES[metric].min) — stored on the step and written to the trail (kind evaluation, with baseline, result, periods, outcome); never re-evaluated. GET /track → {steps, evaluated, needed 5, score {met, of, share} only from 5 evaluated (inconclusive excluded), waitingUntil}. Page: Next steps → "Track record" card (EN/AR): score or "No score yet … Waiting for data until {date}", each step with metric, direction, before → after, periods, outcome, and the attribution limits. Tests: test/track.test.js (5, synthetic before/after GA4 data: plan, met, not met, no change, inconclusive, score threshold, waiting date, trail rows, routes + isolation), ui-tests/track.ui.mjs (EN+AR). test/db.test.js table list. npm test 417/417, npm run test:ui 15/15. Screenshots docs/ui-audit/screenshots-p6/ (the scored card uses five injected earlier steps with artificial low baselines, evaluated against the synthetic FlowNest data).
- **P5 fixes (owner 2026-11-20, on feat/p5-audit-trail).** (1) Strategy approval waits for the server: stratOk = approved && k.stratTrail (the trail id the server returned) && no fact gate; A.stratApprove POSTs approve-strategy first, the button shows "Recording the approval…" (disabled) while it runs; on failure the campaign stays not approved, a toast and an error line appear in Check & download with "Try again" (409 = a linked fact is no longer approved). Generate assets and Create content no longer approve by themselves: they call the same server approval. Campaigns approved before this fix have no stratTrail and must be approved once more. ui-tests/strategy-server.ui.mjs (EN+AR) simulates a slow then failed request and checks downloads stay blocked, then Try again opens them. (2) Recommendation titles are stored in English and Arabic (detail.title {en, ar}; older rows keep a string). (3) Activity is in Settings only: Next steps → Tasks → History and Home "Recent activity" show a "See activity in Settings" link (A.seeAct); browser notes (c.activity) are no longer shown anywhere (still kept in the state). npm test 412/412, npm run test:ui 14/14.
- **TASK FOR P3 (owner 2026-11-20): move the AI Director's rules engine to the server, so the audit trail records answers the server produced, not answers the page reported.** Today, without an AI key, director(c,q) runs in public/index.html and the page sends the answer to POST /api/companies/:id/trail/answer (engine "rules", recorded as reported by the page). Do it together with P3: build the rules answer in server code from buildContext (same period), return it from POST /director/ask when no model is configured, record it in the trail there, and remove /trail/answer (or keep it only for the owner's local endpoint).
- **P5 DONE (2026-11-20, branch feat/p5-audit-trail from feat/p4-fact-approvals 3e0c3bc; no PR yet, owner rule).** server/trail.js: table trail (id, company_id, at, kind, subject, user_id, user_email at that moment, engine, prompt_version, data_hash, batch, detail JSON) + trail_marks (last recorded fingerprint per company and period). Append-only: triggers refuse UPDATE, an INSERT that would replace a row (INSERT OR REPLACE skips delete triggers) and DELETE unless the company itself is being deleted (cascade). Kinds: recommendation (one row per item, written only when the fingerprint of data_mirror hash + fact approvals + approved facts + period + RULES_VERSION + year + items changes; GET /audit sends header X-Trail-Batch, body unchanged), answer (AI: engine = model id, prompt_version = director.PROMPT_VERSION, support ids; rules engine in the page: POST /api/companies/:id/trail/answer, engine rules + RULES_VERSION; local endpoint: engine "local endpoint"), fact_decision (approve / reject / sent back to review / demo expiry, with review date and the fact text; one batch per request), tracking_started (legacy fact ids), strategy_approval (POST /api/companies/:id/campaigns/:cid/approve-strategy {name, factIds}: 409 facts_not_approved if a fact is not approved now; called by stratApprove, Generate assets and Create content), report (reports.save: the snapshot stores trail {batch, ids per recommendation, report}; the list entry has trailId, null for older reports). GET /api/companies/:id/trail?lang&limit&kind&batch (newest first, summaries EN/AR). Page: Settings → "Activity (audit trail)" card read from GET /trail (who, engine / model, prompt or rules version, data fingerprint; batches grouped into one line; <bdi> for mixed-direction text); the same card replaces the browser list under Next steps → Tasks → History; the old browser notes stay in a collapsed "not part of the audit trail" list. Tests: test/trail.test.js (9: triggers, recommendation fingerprints, AI + rules answers, fact decisions, strategy approvals, report trail ids, per-company isolation, every declared route called with the earlier rows unchanged, pre-P5 database), ui-tests/trail.ui.mjs (EN+AR). test/db.test.js table list and test/reports.test.js (trailId) updated. npm test 412/412, npm run test:ui 13/13. Screenshots docs/ui-audit/screenshots-p5/.
