## Verified complete
- Phase 0: Node + Express app serves the frontend and `GET /api/health`.
- Phase 1: `server/db.js` (SQLite schema), `server/auth.js` (register / login / logout / me, scrypt, hashed session tokens, rate limiting), `server/companies.js` (create / list / get / delete, `requireCompany` isolation, per-company state blob `GET`/`PUT .../state`), frontend sign-in card (EN/AR) and server sync in `public/index.html`.
- Phase 2 (exit criteria met, see Last tests):
  - 2.1 knowledge flow works through the real UI with the server sync (edit basics, paste / upload sources, discovery, approve / reject, publish, reload, Arabic RTL).
  - 2.2 `server/knowledge.js` + tables `sources`, `facts`: `PUT .../state` saves blob, name and a validated mirror in one transaction → `{ ok, updatedAt, knowledge: { sources, facts, skipped, downgraded } }`. `GET /api/companies/:id/facts[?status=pending|approved|rejected]` → `{ facts: [{ id, cat, text, type, status, src, srcTitle, synthetic, ev, needs, date }] }`. `approvedFacts(db, companyId)` exported.
  - 2.3 evidence invariant: mirrored as `approved` only with non-blank `ev` or `type: 'user'`; otherwise stored `pending` and returned in `knowledge.downgraded`; the frontend (`syncDowngrade`) returns those to review with an EN/AR toast and a badge. Facts from `demo` sources carry `synthetic: true`.
  - 2.4 uploads and pasted text go through `addText` in `public/index.html`: empty / whitespace / binary content refused with a message; PDF / Office files registered as "text not readable" (never "Readable"); text over 200,000 chars (`SRC_MAX`) cut and marked; text files over 5 MB refused.
  - 2.5 `server/fetchUrl.js` + `POST /api/companies/:id/fetch-url` `{ url }` → `{ url, title, text }`: http/https, ports 80/443/8080/8443, host must resolve only to public addresses (checked on every redirect hop, max 3), connection pinned to the checked address, text content only, 1 MB, 10 s, 20 reads per 10 min per user. Errors: 400 `invalid_url` / `blocked_host`, 413, 415 `unsupported_type`, 429, 502 `fetch_failed`, 504 `timeout`. The "Try to read the website" button uses it.

- Phase 3 (exit criteria met, see Last tests), tasks 3.1–3.7:
- Task 3.1 — import → analytics → audit works through the real UI with the server sync (templates, CSV / JSON import per kind, 12 Analytics KPIs with period selector, Audit with evidence and "Not enough data" labels, reload, Arabic). Fixed: impossible dates crashed Analytics (`realDate` in `importData`). Deferred: the client still accepts negative numbers and does not say why rows were skipped (→ 3.6); re-importing a file doubles its rows (→ 3.6); demo loaders are unreachable because nothing sets `demo` on a company (labeling itself works).
- Task 3.2 — server data mirror: `server/data.js` + tables `data_rows` (company-scoped; kind, date, validated row JSON, synthetic) and `data_mirror` (fingerprint so unchanged saves skip the rewrite). `PUT .../state` now also answers `data: { rows: { meta, ga4, gsc, leads }, skipped }`, in the same transaction as blob and facts. A row is stored only with a real calendar date, all required columns non-empty and numeric columns as finite numbers >= 0; only schema columns are kept (`SCHEMAS`, ported from the frontend `SCH`), strings capped at 500 chars; optional numbers that are absent stay absent (not 0). Rows of a dataset are `synthetic` when the import log has a `fixture` import of that kind. `GET /api/companies/:id/data/summary` → per kind `{ rows, from, to, synthetic }`. `dataRows(db, companyId, kind)` is the only read path for imported data on the server.
- Task 3.3 — server KPIs: `server/metrics.js` (`computeMetrics(data, range)`, `metrics(db, companyId, range)`), reading only `dataRows`. `GET /api/companies/:id/metrics?range=7|28|90|all` (default 28; 400 `invalid_range`) → `{ range, period: { from, to, days } | null, previous: { from, to } | null, synthetic, kpis }`. 16 KPIs: `reach`, `impressions`, `engagements`, `link_clicks`, `spend`, `sessions`, `enquiries`, `enquiry_rate`, `search_clicks`, `search_impressions`, `ctr`, `leads`, `qualified_leads`, `qualified_share`, `customers`, `cost_per_lead`; each `{ value, rows, synthetic, previous }`, and `value: null` + `reason: 'no_data' | 'not_measured'` when nothing supports it (never 0). Rates are fractions. Period rules copy the Analytics screen: ends at the latest data date, clamped to the first, previous period only when it fits.
- Task 3.4 — server audit: `server/audit.js` (`computeAudit(data, facts)`, `audit(db, companyId)`), reading only `dataRows` and `approvedFacts`. `GET /api/companies/:id/audit` → `{ period: { from, to } | null, synthetic, items }`; item = `{ id, kind: strong|weak|opp|risk|gap, basis: 'data' | 'facts' | 'insufficient', synthetic, title, why, action, evidence: [{ text, metric?, value?, rows?, factId? }], needs? }`. `insufficient` items are not conclusions: they say which input is missing or too small. Ported from the prototype with the same ids and thresholds (exported as `T`): `no-ga4`, `no-track`, `ch-best`, `ch-leak`, `mobile-gap`, `no-gsc`, `seo-q`, `no-leads`, `src-unknown`, `src-best`, `src-lowq`, `no-meta`, `brand-doc`. **Not ported yet — superseded, see Task 6.11 for the current browser-only list (`seg-best` was ported in 5.4b, `page-leak` and `theme-best` in 5.7, `cadence`, `eng-drop`, `lang`, `plat-roles`, `no-spend` in 6.11):** `defs`, `plat-roles`, `theme-best`, `cadence`, `eng-drop`, `lang` and the document-observation rules (`cta-mix`, `offer-conflict`, `claims`, `prod-consistent`, `know-gaps`). Server text is English only; the bilingual wording stays in the frontend, keyed by the same rule ids. The audit looks at all rows (like the prototype), not one period.
- Task 3.5 — the Marketing Audit view shows the server check (frontend only, `public/index.html`): `srvAudit(c)` fetches `GET .../audit` when the view renders and the cached answer does not match the last saved state (`SRV.audit[companyId] = { key: SYNC.sent[id], by, items }`, memory only, never in the company state); `syncNow` re-renders the Audit view after a successful `PUT`, which triggers the refetch. `recCard(c,x,sb)` takes an optional badge row (only `V.audit` passes it; dashboard / growth cards are unchanged): `srvBadge` → "Verified on the server from your data" / "…from approved facts" / "Not enough data" + what is needed / "Computed in your browser — not yet verified on the server", plus "Synthetic" when the server item is synthetic. Server `insufficient` items the browser audit does not show are listed under "Not enough data yet" (tabs All and Risks & gaps). Arabic titles / needs for the nine `insufficient` ids are in `SRV_AR` (static wording: it does not carry the server's live counts). While loading: "Checking these findings on the server…"; on failure: "Server check unavailable…" and the browser audit as before (retry after 10 s on the next render). Not covered: the exported audit (MD) and the dashboard / Growth cards carry no server badge.
- Task 3.6 — import honesty (frontend only, `importData` in `public/index.html`): a row is skipped for an impossible / missing date, an empty required value, a non-finite number or a negative number — the same rules as the server mirror — and the counts are stored on the import record (`skipped` = total, `why = { date, required, number, negative }`) and shown in the toast and the Import history (EN/AR). The same file content imported again for the same dataset is refused ("already imported… Nothing was changed"; fingerprint `sig` on the import record; imports made before 3.6 have no fingerprint; removing a dataset's data also removes its import records, so the file can then be imported again). Each dataset holds at most `ROW_MAX` = 5,000 rows (4 kinds must fit the 5 MB state limit): extra rows are not imported and counted in `capped`; a full dataset refuses further imports. This resolves the 3.1 deferrals (negatives, skip reasons, double-counting). Still as before: an empty optional number is stored as 0 by the browser.
- Task 4.1 — AI Director grounding context, no AI call: `server/director.js` — `buildContext(db, companyId)` (null for an unknown company) and pure `shapeContext`, reading only `approvedFacts`, `metrics(…, 'all')` and `audit`. `GET /api/companies/:id/director/context` → `{ company: { name }, facts: [{ id, cat, text, ev, enteredManually, srcTitle, synthetic }], metrics: { range, period, synthetic, kpis: { <name>: { value, rows, synthetic } | { value: null, reason, synthetic } } }, audit: { period, synthetic, items: [{ id, kind, basis, synthetic, title, why, action, evidence, needs? }] }, synthetic, truncated?: { facts } }`. Caps (`CAPS`): 200 facts, fact text 500 chars, evidence 300 chars, 10 evidence lines per audit item. Facts typed in by the user carry `enteredManually: true` and no quote. **This object is the only material a prompt may be built from (4.3).**
- Task 4.2 — AI client: provider = **Claude (Anthropic)**, owner's choice 2026-11-12. `server/ai.js`: `createAi({ apiKey, model, fetch, timeoutMs, maxRetries })` → `{ configured, model, complete({ system, messages, maxTokens = 16000, effort }) → { text, model, stopReason, truncated } }`, through the official `@anthropic-ai/sdk` (1.8.0; `client.beta.messages.create` with server-side refusal fallback `fallbacks: 'default'`, beta `server-side-fallback-2026-07-01`). Key from `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`, model from `AI_MODEL` (default `claude-opus-5-5`); no sampling parameters, no `thinking` parameter (adaptive is the model default). Errors are `AiError` with `code` / `status`: `ai_not_configured` 503, `ai_auth_failed` 502, `ai_rate_limited` 429, `ai_timeout` 504, `ai_refused` 422, `ai_failed` 502 — the provider's body and the key never reach a response. `createApp({ ai })` (default from the environment) → `app.locals.ai`; `GET /api/ai/status` (signed in) → `{ configured }`. `server/index.js` loads a git-ignored `.env` when present (`process.loadEnvFile`); `.env.example` added. **Not done: no real call to Claude has been made** (no key on this machine) — everything is verified against a stubbed network.
- Task 4.3 — grounded question, server only: `server/director.js` gains `ask(db, ai, companyId, { question, lang })` plus pure `buildPrompt`, `parseReply`, `checkSupport`, `isEmptyContext`, limits `ASK` (question 1,000 chars, answer 8,000, 30 citations). `POST /api/companies/:id/director/ask` `{ question, lang?: 'en' | 'ar' (default en) }` behind `requireCompany` → `{ answer, support: [{ type: 'fact' | 'metric' | 'audit', id, text, synthetic }], insufficient, unsupported, synthetic, model }`. Order of checks: 400 `invalid_question` / `invalid_lang` → per-user limit (20 per 10 min, `createApp({ askLimit })`) 429 `too_many_attempts` → 503 `ai_not_configured` → context. A context with no facts, no KPI value and no `data` / `facts` audit item answers `{ answer: null, insufficient: true, model: null, needs: [strings from the audit items] }` **without a model call**. Otherwise one `ai.complete` (default `maxTokens`, no effort set): the system prompt fixes role, language, context-only, data-is-not-instructions, cite ids, say when it cannot answer, JSON-only reply; the user message is `<context>` JSON + `<question>` (JSON string), with `<` escaped so company text cannot close a tag. The reply is read as bare JSON, a code fence or JSON inside prose; citations are kept only when the id exists in this company's context (fact id, KPI name, audit id), de-duplicated, and their `text` / `synthetic` come from the context, never from the model. `unsupported: true` = not `insufficient` and no citation survived (the UI must label it). Plain prose with no JSON → labeled answer (`unsupported`); empty, cut (`max_tokens`), broken JSON or no `answer` string → 502 `ai_failed`. `AiError` → its status and `{ error: code }`; the cause's message goes to `console.error` only. Decisions: a cited KPI with a null value is a valid citation ("not available"); an `insufficient` audit item is too. **Not done: no real call to Claude** — how a real model follows the prompt (clean JSON, citations, Arabic) is untested until 4.5. The server checks that cited ids exist, not that the answer's wording actually follows from them.
- Task 4.4 — the Marketing Director screen uses the server AI (frontend only, `public/index.html`). `aiOn()` / `aiStatus()` read `GET /api/ai/status` once per page load into `SRV.ai` (memory only). The `ask` action: when the user's own local endpoint mode is not set and `SRV.ai` is true, it shows the question with "Asking the AI Marketing Director…" (`SRV.asking`, memory only — one question at a time), waits until the server holds the browser's latest state (`synced(c)`), then calls `POST .../director/ask` with `lang: S.lang` and stores the result in `c.chat` through `aiAnswer(r)`. Chat messages now carry `a.src`: `'ai'` → badge "AI answer", title "Based on your approved facts and data", answer text escaped, then "Supporting evidence — checked on the server" (type label + server text, "Synthetic" badge per synthetic item), a synthetic-data line when `r.synthetic`, a warning box "No supporting evidence found — treat this answer as unverified" when `unsupported` (badge "Hypothesis"), and a warning when the model reports `insufficient`; `'none'` → "Not enough data to answer" with the server's `needs` list and two buttons (Company sources, Integrations & Data), no AI label; `'rules'` → badge "Rules engine — not AI" on every rules-engine answer. Fallback to the rules engine with a one-line reason: any failed request ("The AI was unavailable…"), 429 ("Too many AI questions…"), state not saved ("…could not be saved to the server, so the AI was not asked"); 503 switches `SRV.ai` off silently. With AI on, the screen shows a green note saying which material is sent to the AI model. All texts EN/AR. Unchanged: rules / bridge / endpoint modes (a configured local endpoint still takes priority), "Export context" / "Import Claude answer". Known limits: `needs` and `support[].text` (audit titles, `kpi_name: value`) are the server's English wording also in Arabic; a stored answer keeps the language it was asked in; the header badge still reads "Rules engine · offline" when the AI is on; answers imported before 4.4 have no source badge.
- Phase 5 (exit criteria met, see Last tests), tasks 5.1–5.6:
- Task 5.1 — the prototype's campaign flow works through the real UI with the server sync (no new server code). Flow as it is today, all in the browser from the state blob: Audit card "Create campaign from this" (`recCamp`) → `newCampaign(c, rec)` stores `camp.rec` = the recommendation id and copies its evidence into `brief.why`; the brief is filled only from approved findings (`K(cat, c)`), per language, and anything missing is left blank and listed ("Not found in approved knowledge"); "Approve strategy and generate assets" (`genAssets`) → 12 template assets (3 posts, landing page, email, messaging guide × EN / AR), a missing slot shows as `⟦slot⟧`; fields are edited in the Assets tab (marked "edited", new version); Change impact (`applyChanges`) rewrites affected assets and keeps hand edits; Brand QA (`qa(c, camp)`, 10 checks) gates "Approve all"; Export & Delivery (`ex`) gives a zip (32 files incl. `strategy.md`, `brand-qa-report.md`, `campaign.json`), strategy, calendar, campaign JSON. **Fixed:** an English banner previewed while the interface is in Arabic was laid out right-to-left and cut off (`bannerSVG` now sets `direction="ltr"` for English). **Gaps found, queued:** (a) the server knows nothing about campaigns — no validated mirror, so nothing on the server can say a campaign's CTA / offer / claims are approved (→ 5.2, 5.3); (b) export is allowed while QA checks fail or assets are unapproved, and the package does not say so apart from `brand-qa-report.md` (→ 5.4); (c) "generate" is enabled with an empty brief and produces assets made of `⟦…⟧` markers (QA flags them, export still works) (→ 5.4); (d) the recommendation link is an id only: neither the Studio nor `strategy.md` names the recommendation, and the id is not checked against the server audit (`seg-best` is not ported to the server) (→ 5.3, 5.5); (e) the optional fourth landing feature shows `⟦p4⟧` in the editor and in `campaign.json` when only three proof points are approved (QA exempts it; the landing page hides it) — cosmetic, not queued.
- Task 5.2 — server campaign mirror: `server/campaigns.js` (`createCampaigns(db)` → `mirror`, `list`, `get`; pure `cleanCampaign`, `cleanAsset`, `cleanBrief`; `LIMITS`) + tables `campaigns`, `campaign_assets` (cascade from the company and from the campaign), `campaign_mirror` (fingerprint: an unchanged `state.campaigns` skips the rewrite). `PUT .../state` now also answers `campaigns: { campaigns, assets, skipped }`, in the same transaction as blob, facts and data. Kept per campaign: `id`, `name` (1–200), `slug` (`a-z0-9-`, ≤ 80), `rec` (id or null), `approved` (only literal `true`), `createdAt` (from `at`), brief = 13 text fields (≤ 1,000 chars, wrong type → `''`), `why` / `pointsEn` / `pointsAr` (≤ 10 strings), `channels` (facebook / instagram / linkedin / email); per asset: `id`, `type` (post / landing / email / guide), `lang` (en / ar), `channel` (the four + web / internal), `n`, `status` (anything but `approved` is `draft`), `flagged`, fields `{ key: { v (≤ 20,000), edited } }`. Limits: 50 campaigns, 60 assets per campaign, 30 fields per asset. A campaign with a bad id / name / slug / rec, a duplicate id, an asset with an unknown type / lang / channel or no usable field, and a malformed field are skipped and counted. **Not mirrored:** design, colours, font, versions, target, uploaded images, templates (`tpl`). Routes: `GET /api/companies/:id/campaigns` → `{ campaigns: [{ id, name, slug, rec, approved, createdAt, assets, approvedAssets }] }`; `GET /api/companies/:id/campaigns/:cid` → `{ campaign: { …, brief, assets: [{ id, type, lang, channel, n, status, flagged, fields }] } }`, 404 `not_found`. Read path for server code: `campaigns(db, companyId)`. Deviation from the handoff spec: `createdAt` instead of `updatedAt` (the mirror is rewritten as a whole, so a per-campaign update time would be invented). Companies saved before 5.2 get their campaign rows on their next save.
- Task 5.3 — server campaign checks: `server/campaignQa.js` (pure `computeCampaignQa(campaign, facts, auditItems)`, `campaignQa(db, companyId, campaignId)`, exported `RISK`, `NOT_CHECKED`), reading only the campaign mirror, `approvedFacts` and `audit`. `GET /api/companies/:id/campaigns/:cid/qa` → `{ campaign: { id, name, rec }, checks: [{ id, pass, basis: 'facts' | 'campaign' | 'audit', affected: [asset ids | 'brief'], detail }], passed, failed, exportable, notChecked: ['contrast', 'palette'] }`, 404 `not_found`. Ten checks, in this order: `cta-used`, `cta-approved` (each brief CTA is the trimmed text of an approved `ctas` fact), `offer-approved` (same for `offers` — **new, the browser has no such check**), `offer-numbers`, `claims`, `placeholders` (field `f4` exempt), `language`, `links`, `review`, `recommendation` (`rec` null → pass "Manual campaign"; `rec` among the server audit's `data` / `facts` items → pass; otherwise fail "not verified on the server" — the case today for `seg-best`, `page-leak`, `theme-best`, which the server audit does not compute). `exportable` = no failed check, at least one asset, every asset `approved`. Same rules and patterns as the browser's `qa(c, camp)` for the seven ported checks; contrast and palette stay browser-only (colours are not mirrored). Detail text is English only. The route recomputes on every call (no cache). Consequence to know for 5.4: a campaign created from the segment recommendation (the only campaign-making rule that fired in the 5.1 run) currently fails `recommendation` on the server.
- Task 5.4 — Brand QA shows the server's verdicts; exports are honest (frontend only, `public/index.html`). `qa(c, camp)` checks now carry ids (`cta-used`, `cta-approved`, `offer-numbers`, `claims`, `placeholders`, `language`, `contrast`, `palette`, `links`, `review`). `srvQa(c, camp)` / `srvQaLoad` fetch `GET .../campaigns/:cid/qa` when the QA view renders (cache `SRV.qa['company/campaign']`, keyed by the last saved state, memory only; `syncNow` re-renders the QA view after a save; retry 10 s after a failure). `qaRows(Q, sv)` merges both: the server verdict wins; each row is tagged "Verified on the server" (both agree), "Browser and server disagree: it fails / passes on the server" (+ the server's detail, escaped), or "Checked in your browser only" (contrast, palette); two server-only rows are added — "Campaign offer is approved in the Knowledge Hub" and "The recommendation behind this campaign is verified on the server" (tag "Checked on the server"). The header count and "Approve all" use the merged rows; a failed recommendation row does **not** lock "Approve all" (`nogate`). While loading: "Checking this campaign on the server…"; on failure: "Server check unavailable — … Exports are labeled DRAFT…". **Export:** the zip action first saves (`synced`) and asks the server afresh (`srvQaNow`); `draftLine(c, k, sv)` → when the server is unavailable, any check fails (server checks + browser-only contrast / palette) or any asset is unapproved, the file name ends `-DRAFT.zip`, `README.txt` and `brand-qa-report.md` start with "DRAFT — not approved: n check(s) failing, m asset(s) not approved." (or "DRAFT — not verified: the server check was unavailable…"), and a red toast says so (EN/AR); `brand-qa-report.md` always ends with "## Server checks" (or "unavailable at export time"). A single unapproved asset downloads as `…-DRAFT.<ext>` with a toast. **Generate guard:** `gen` asks for confirmation when CTA / offer / main message (EN or AR) or three proof points per language are missing. Known limits: a campaign made from the segment recommendation always exports as DRAFT until 5.4b (its recommendation is not verified on the server); the other exports (strategy, calendar, campaign JSON) carry no DRAFT label; server `detail` text is English; Change impact sets changed assets back to draft (existing behaviour), so a package after a change is DRAFT until they are approved again.
- Task 5.4b — `seg-best` on the server: `server/audit.js` now computes the segment rule from validated lead rows with the browser's rule and numbers (segments with 15+ leads, qualified = `qualified` or `customer`, fires when there are two or more such segments and the best one's qualified share is above 115% of the overall share; thresholds `T.segmentMinLeads`, `T.segmentOverAverage`, `T.segmentSmallSample`); with leads but fewer than two sized segments it returns an `insufficient` item (`needs`: two segments with 15+ leads each). Effects: the Audit card for the segment recommendation shows "Verified on the server from your data"; a campaign made from it passes the server `recommendation` check and, once approved, exports as a final package; the item is part of the AI Director context. The "not verified" wording in Brand QA now covers both reasons (rule runs only in the browser, or the data changed since the campaign was created). Arabic title / needs for the new `insufficient` id added to `SRV_AR`. **Still browser-only:** `page-leak` and `theme-best` (the other two campaign-making rules) — campaigns made from them fail `recommendation` on the server and export as DRAFT.
- Task 5.5 — the recommendation behind a campaign is named (frontend only): `newCampaign` stores `camp.recTitle` (the recommendation's title when the campaign was created, in the language used then); `recLine(c, camp)` shows, in the brief's "Why this campaign" card, "From the recommendation: <title>" with an "Open in Marketing Audit" button, plus "This recommendation is no longer produced by your current data." when the browser audit no longer returns that rule; campaigns made before 5.5 get the title looked up by rule id (else the id); manual campaigns keep "Created manually". `strategy.md` (`strategyMD`) starts its evidence section with "Recommendation: <title> (rule <id>)" or "Created manually — no recommendation."; `campaign.json` carries `rec` and `recTitle`. Not done: `recTitle` is not in the server mirror (it would need a new column; the server already checks the rule id against its own audit).
- Task 5.7 — two more audit rules on the server (`server/audit.js`): `page-leak` (GA4 rows with `landing_page`: a page carrying 12%+ of sessions with an enquiry rate under 60% of the site average; the one with most sessions; `insufficient` when no row has a landing page) and `theme-best` (Meta rows with `theme`, posts tagged `campaign` excluded from themes: themes with 4+ posts, fires when there are two or more and the best one's engagement ÷ reach is above 120% of the account average; `insufficient` otherwise). Thresholds `T.pageMinShare`, `T.pageBelowAverage`, `T.themeMinPosts`, `T.themeOverAverage`. Same rules and numbers as the browser; the server writes plain numbers ("1233 sessions") where the browser writes "1,233", and straight quotes where the browser uses curly ones. Arabic wording for the two new `insufficient` ids in `SRV_AR`. **All four campaign-making data rules (`ch-best`, `ch-leak`, `theme-best`, `seg-best`) are now verified on the server.** Correction of an earlier note: `page-leak` proposes an experiment, not a campaign. Still browser-only (as of 5.7; see Task 6.11 for the current list): the portfolio rules (`pf-…`, from project documents) — a campaign made from one fails the server `recommendation` check and exports as DRAFT; and `defs`, `plat-roles`, `cadence`, `eng-drop`, `lang`, the document-observation rules.
- Task 5.8 — two small fixes. (1) `server/index.js`: a listen failure is no longer swallowed — the app prints "BrandPilot could not start on port N: the port is already in use / the port is not permitted (it may be in a range reserved by the system). Choose another port with the PORT variable, for example PORT=4000." and exits with code 1; "running" is printed only on success. (2) `public/index.html`: the header badge reads "AI Director on · rules engine for analysis" (with a matching tooltip, EN/AR) when the server AI is configured and the AI mode is "rules"; `aiStatus()` is now called from the header, so the status is fetched on any screen and the page re-renders once when the AI turns out to be on. With the AI off the badge is unchanged ("Rules engine · offline").
- Task 6.1 — server marketing report: `server/report.js` — `buildReport(db, companyId, { range, now })` (null for an unknown company) and `renderReport(report, lang)`, reading only `metrics`, `audit`, `approvedFacts` and the campaign list. `GET /api/companies/:id/report?range=7|28|90|all&format=json|md&lang=en|ar` (defaults 28 / json / en; 400 `invalid_range` / `invalid_format` / `invalid_lang`). JSON: `{ company, generatedAt, range, period, previous, synthetic, kpis: [{ id, format: number|percent|money, value, previous, change, reason?, synthetic }], findings: { strong, weak, opp, risk: [{ id, basis, synthetic, title, why, action, evidence[] }] }, gaps: [{ id, title, needs }], knowledge: { approvedFacts, synthetic }, campaigns: [{ id, name, approved, assets, approvedAssets, rec }] }`. `format=md` downloads `marketing-report-<date>.md` (labels EN / AR; company text is escaped). A KPI without support is written "no data" / "not measured", never 0; `insufficient` audit items appear only under "Not enough data yet"; synthetic data is labeled. **Limits:** finding texts are English only (the Arabic report says so); spend has no currency label; the app shows it on Export & Delivery (6.2).
- Task 6.2 — the report in the app (frontend only, `public/index.html`): Export & Delivery has a "Marketing report" card (`repCard(c)`): period tabs 7 / 28 / 90 / all (`repRange`), "Preview" (`repPreview`: waits for `synced(c)`, fetches `GET .../report?range=`, renders period, KPI table with labels from `REP_K`, "No data" / "Not measured" for null values, signed change, findings by section with basis, evidence and action, "Not enough data yet" with Arabic wording from `SRV_AR`, approved-facts count, campaigns) and "Download (MD)" (`repDl`: `format=md&lang=<S.lang>`, saved through `dl`). State lives in `SRV.rep[companyId] = { range, st: idle|load|ok|err, data, key }`, memory only. A preview built before the latest save shows "Your data changed after this preview was built — refresh it" (`syncNow` re-renders the Export view after a save when a preview is shown). A failed request shows a note / toast; the other exports are untouched. In Arabic the card says the finding texts are English.
- Task 6.3 — printable report: `renderReportHtml(report, lang)` in `server/report.js` — one self-contained page (inline CSS, no scripts, no links, no external requests; `lang` / `dir="rtl"` for Arabic; numbers and dates kept left-to-right; `@page` A4 and `@media print` rules; every company / audit text HTML-escaped). `GET .../report?format=html&lang=` answers `text/html` with `Content-Security-Policy: default-src 'none'; style-src 'unsafe-inline'; base-uri 'none'; form-action 'none'`, `nosniff`, `no-store`. The report card has "Open printable report" (`repPrint`): the tab is opened at once and pointed at the page after `synced(c)`; a blocked pop-up or a failed save shows a message. The user prints or saves as PDF from the browser — there is no PDF library.
- Task 6.4 — report history: table `reports` (`id`, `company_id` cascade, `range`, `lang`, `auto`, `created_at`, `data` = the JSON from `buildReport`) and `server/reports.js` — `createReports(db)` → `save(companyId, { range, lang, auto, now })` (builds now, stores, keeps the newest `REPORTS_MAX` = 50 per company in one transaction; null for an unknown company), `list(companyId)` (newest first, no bodies: `{ id, range, lang, auto, createdAt, period, synthetic }`), `get(companyId, id)` (`+ report`). Routes: `POST /api/companies/:id/reports` `{ range, lang }` → 201 `{ report }` (30 per 10 min per user → 429; `createApp({ reportLimit })`), `GET .../reports` → `{ reports }`, `GET .../reports/:rid?format=json|md|html` → the stored snapshot, rendered in the language it was saved in, **never rebuilt**; 404 `not_found` for an unknown id or another company's id. `sendReport` in `server/companies.js` serves both the live and the saved report. App: the report card has "Save this report" (`repSave`) and a "Saved reports" table (`repHist`, fetched when the Export view opens, memory only) with "Open" (`repOpen`, printable page) and "Download (MD)" (`repGet`), an "Automatic" tag when `auto`.
- Task 6.5 — scheduled reports (no email): table `report_schedules` (`company_id` primary key with cascade, `every`, `range`, `lang`, `next_due`, `updated_at`; no row = off). `server/reports.js`: `getSchedule(companyId)`, `setSchedule(companyId, { every: off|weekly|monthly, lang }, now)` (weekly → range `7`, monthly → `28`; first report due one interval from now; changing only the language keeps the due date), `runDue(now)` (saves one `auto` snapshot per due company, moves `next_due` past `now` so a long downtime gives one report, isolates failures per company, returns the count), `addInterval`, `SCHEDULES`. Routes: `GET` / `PUT /api/companies/:id/reports/schedule` (`{ every, lang }`; 400 `invalid_schedule` / `invalid_lang`), declared before `/:rid`. Timer only in `server/index.js`: `runDue` once at start, then hourly (`setInterval(...).unref()`); `createApp` starts no timers. App: "Automatic report" Off / Weekly / Monthly (`repSched`, saved in the interface language) with "Next report due: …"; automatic snapshots carry the "Automatic" tag. **Limits:** times are UTC-based intervals; a report is saved only while the server process is running (checked hourly); nothing is emailed — that needs an SMTP account from the owner.
- Task 6.7 — deployment readiness (`server/app.js`): `TRUST_PROXY` (env, or `createApp({ trustProxy })`; `parseTrustProxy`: empty / 0 / false → off, a number → that many proxies, `true` → 1) sets Express `trust proxy`, so behind a reverse proxy the login limit keys on the visitor's address — and without it a forged `X-Forwarded-For` is ignored; every response carries `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`, `X-Frame-Options: DENY`, `Referrer-Policy: same-origin`; `public/favicon.svg` is linked from the page and also answers `/favicon.ico` (no more 404). `.env.example` documents `TRUST_PROXY`. **Not done:** no Content-Security-Policy for the main app (it is one file of inline script and styles; the printable report has a strict one); HTTPS is the proxy's job; rate limiters are still in-memory per process.
- Task 6.8 — currency in reports: table `company_settings` (`company_id` primary key with cascade, `currency`); on every `PUT .../state` the server stores `state.currency` when it is a 3-letter uppercase code, else null (same transaction as the other mirrors). `buildReport` returns `currency`; money KPIs (`spend`, `cost_per_lead`) are written "4,200 EGP" in the Markdown, the printable page and the app preview, and as plain numbers when there is no currency; snapshots saved earlier have no `currency` and still render. The browser sets `c.currency = 'EGP'` when a company has none (`coBody`), matching the "EGP" the prototype screens hard-code. **Limit:** there is no currency selector in the app — every company is EGP until one is added. Also: the saved-report date range no longer wraps (`white-space: nowrap`).
- Task 6.9 — Arabic findings on the server: `computeAudit(data, facts, { lang })` and `audit(db, companyId, { lang })` (`en` default, `ar`): one rule implementation, every wording literal written as `L(english, arabic)` inside `server/audit.js` — titles, reasons, actions, evidence lines and `needs`, for all 15 rule ids; ids, kinds, basis, numbers and evidence `metric` / `value` / `rows` are identical in both languages; an approved fact's own text is quoted as it is; numbers stay Western digits. `GET .../audit?lang=en|ar` (400 `invalid_lang`). `buildReport(…, { lang })` builds findings and gaps in that language and records `findingsLang`; `GET .../report?lang=` applies to JSON too; saved and automatic reports use the language they were saved in. The "finding texts are English" note is printed only for Arabic renderings of reports saved before this (no `findingsLang`). App: the report preview asks for `lang=<S.lang>` and, after a language switch, says the findings are in the other language until refreshed; gap wording now comes from the server. **Unchanged:** English output (all earlier tests pass as they were); the AI Director context and campaign QA stay English; the Marketing Audit screen still writes its own bilingual cards in the browser. The Arabic sentences were written for this task (close to, not copied from, the prototype's) and have not been reviewed by a native-speaking marketer.
- Task 6.10b — six fixes from the 2026-11-12 full-cycle run (`public/index.html` unless noted). (1) `headCat(h)`: a source heading that opens with "why" / «لماذا» is filed under `diff` (when a differentiation keyword is present) or `value`; every other heading keeps the `CATS` order. The fact editor (`kSave` form) has a category select; moving a fact marks it edited, sets `kdirty`, and drops `primary` when it leaves `ctas`. (2) "Export context for Claude" (`copyPkg`) puts in `question` the text in the "Ask a question…" box, else the last question asked (imported answers are skipped: chat entries from `claudeSave` carry `imp: true`), else `""`. (3) Imported advice (`extAnswer`) shows an "Add as improvement task" button under **every** recommendation with the note "External advice — the task is marked unverified" (before: "Add task: …" buttons for the first four only, at the end of the message); a second click on the same advice adds nothing; tasks keep the "Claude import · unverified" badge. Imported advice has no campaign button (by design: nothing imported can become a server-verified campaign). (4) `fill`: an English field that opens with a slot starts with a capital (not for `name`, `cta`, `link`; Arabic untouched), and a slot value that already ends with `. ! ? ؟ …` does not get the template's full stop as well. Assets generated earlier keep their text until regenerated. (5) Bidi: `bidi(s)` exported from `server/report.js` and `bd(s)` in the frontend (same pattern) escape the text and wrap each Latin / numeric run in `<bdi>`; used only when the page / interface is Arabic — printable report (finding title, reason, evidence, action, gaps), report preview (same fields), Marketing Audit cards (title, reason, evidence, action) and the "Not enough data yet" list. A line that starts with a Latin name is now laid out right-to-left (`dir="auto"` ignores `<bdi>`). Markdown is unchanged; English output has no `<bdi>`. (6) The fact editor closes on any navigation (`go`) and on Escape. Also: `.claude/launch.json` has a `brandpilot-check` entry (port 3100, `data/browser-check.db`) for browser runs on this machine.
- Task 6.10 — three prototype quirks (frontend only). (1) `render()` now wraps `render0()`: before re-rendering it records the focused control (`fKey`: tag + `data-c` / `data-k` / `data-a` / `data-v` / `data-s` / `data-i` / `name` / `type`, plus its index among equal matches) and the caret, and afterwards re-focuses the matching new control (`preventScroll`). Because Chrome has already moved focus to `<body>` when `change` fires, a Tab (`keydown`, with Shift) or a `pointerdown` on a focusable control is recorded as an intent (`FINT`, cleared on any normal `focusin`, valid 1.5 s); when focus was lost during the render, focus goes to the next / previous visible focusable control after the one being left, or to the clicked control. Nothing is focused when nothing was. Works for every form that saves on `change`, not only Company basics. (2) `guideHTML` returns nothing unless `c.demo`; the panel padding (`gopen`) also needs `c.demo`. (3) `aiBadge()` (EN / AR): local endpoint set → "Rules + local endpoint"; server AI on → "AI Director on · rules engine for analysis"; bridge → "Rules + Claude import/export"; otherwise "Rules engine" (was "Rules engine · offline"); each tooltip says what produces the analysis and, when there is no server AI, that the AI Marketing Director needs an API key set by the administrator.
- Task 6.11 — five more audit rules on the server (`server/audit.js`), from validated Meta rows, same ids, thresholds and numbers as the browser, wording as `L(english, arabic)`: `cadence` (longest gap between posting days over `T.cadenceMaxGap` = 14 → weak; one posting day only → `insufficient`), `eng-drop` (posts of the last `T.recentDays` = 28 days up to the latest post, campaign posts left out, against all earlier posts; fires when recent engagement ÷ reach is under `T.engagementDrop` = 85% of the earlier rate; fewer than 6 recent or 10 earlier posts → `insufficient`), `lang` (both `ar` and `en` posts present: 5+ each and more than 12% apart → opp; under 5 in either → `insufficient`; one language only → nothing), `plat-roles` (both `facebook` and `instagram` present → opp, action chosen by link-click rate; one platform only → nothing), `no-spend` (Meta rows with no spend at all → `insufficient` "No spend data — cost per lead cannot be calculated"; not on the 6.11 list but the same kind of rule). Decision: a single language or a single platform is a normal state, not missing data, so it gives no gap. Arabic wording for the new server-only gaps `cadence`, `eng-drop`, `lang` added to `SRV_AR` in `public/index.html` (static, without the live counts). English texts equal the browser's word for word; Arabic texts use the plural address form of the other server rules ("اعتمدوا" where the browser says "اعتمد") plus two small wording differences — numbers and titles are identical. **Still browser-only (not portable without new trusted inputs):** `defs` (the "qualified lead" definition is only in the state blob — mirroring it would need a new setting); the document-observation rules `cta-mix`, `offer-conflict`, `claims`, `prod-consistent`, `know-gaps` (they read source documents and pending findings, not trusted server input); the portfolio rules `pf-depth`, `pf-none`, `pf-noout`, `pf-noproof`, `pf-old`, `pf-seg` (project documents). A campaign made from a portfolio rule still fails the server `recommendation` check and exports as DRAFT.
- Task 6.12 — rollout-polish exit check (see Last tests). Three problems found and fixed: (1) **server KPIs** (`server/metrics.js`): Meta `impressions` and `link_clicks` are optional columns; when no row in the period carries one, the KPI is now `value: null, reason: 'not_measured'` (was 0) — so the report, the printable page and the AI context no longer show "0 impressions" for a file without that column; a real 0 in the column stays 0; (2) **Analytics tiles** (`public/index.html`, `hasCol` / `optV` / `optP` beside `kpi`): the same rule in the browser, "Not measured" on the Overview and Facebook / Instagram tabs, no change percentage against a non-number; (3) **phone layout**: under 820 px the page scrolled sideways once a wide table was on screen (saved reports, Integrations import history, Growth tables) because the narrow-screen CSS used `grid-template-columns: 1fr` where the wide rules use `minmax(0,1fr)` — `.app`, `.studio`, `.g21`, `.g2`, `.g3`, `.rec dl` now use `minmax(0,1fr)`, so tables scroll inside their own box; the two long Studio badges (UTM name, "Template drafts…") may wrap. Found, not fixed (queued as 6.13): Change impact cannot change the Arabic audience or proof points. Noted, not a defect: discovery skips lines under a heading that matches no category (the "number one … guaranteed" claim in the test text was not proposed, so it could not be approved by mistake).
- Task 6.13 — Campaign Studio → Change impact (frontend only, `public/index.html`): the form now has Target audience (Arabic) and Proof points (English / Arabic, one per line, up to 10) besides the earlier fields; the `changes` handler sends `audienceAr` (already handled by `applyChanges` through `KEYMAP`) and `pointsEn` / `pointsAr` arrays; `applyChanges` has a new branch for proof points: assets in that language whose template uses `p1`–`p4` are refilled when not hand-edited; a hand-edited field gets each changed point replaced where the old point's text appears (mode `merged`), otherwise it is flagged for review; changed assets go back to draft and the change is a new version with labels from `BLBL` ("Proof points (Arabic, one per line)" …). `ci(k, rows)` now shows arrays one item per line. This resolves the 6.12 finding: a campaign generated before its Arabic wording was filled can be completed without recreating it.
- Task 6.14 — currency selector (frontend only, `public/index.html`): Company basics has "Currency of your spend figures" / «عملة أرقام الإنفاق» (`select data-c="basic" data-k="currency"`: EGP, SAR, AED, KWD, QAR, BHD, OMR, JOD, USD, EUR, GBP; a stored code outside the list stays selectable). Helpers next to `coBody`: `cur(c)` = the company's 3-letter code or EGP, `curA(c)` = the Arabic symbol for EGP / SAR / AED (ج.م، ر.س، د.إ) else the code. The three hard-coded places now use them: Analytics "Spend (…)", Growth & Leads "Spend in period … per lead", campaign learning advice (`campAdvice`). The server already stored `state.currency` (6.8), so reports, the printable page and saved reports follow the choice with no server change. Known difference: server reports write the code ("350 SAR") also in Arabic, while the app's Arabic screens use the symbol.
- Task 6.15 — Arabic bidi isolation everywhere (frontend only, `public/index.html`). (1) `render()` now runs `bidiPass($('#app'))` after `render0()` when the interface is Arabic: every Latin / numeric run in a text node (same `BD` pattern as `bd()` and the server's `bidi()`) is wrapped in `<bdi dir="ltr">`; skipped: text inside `script, style, textarea, select, option, svg, pre, code, bdi, input` and any `[dir="ltr"]` element; English is untouched; attributes are never touched (DOM text nodes only). This covers fixed template text that never went through `bd()` — Dashboard health checks, Analytics notes and comparisons, Growth plan, Director button labels, e.g. "23 عميل, 100% مؤهلون". (2) Director answers (`director()`), AI answers (`aiAnswer`), imported answers (`extAnswer`), the answer title, "Information used" and action-button labels now use `bd()` instead of `esc()` for text (attribute values keep `esc`), so answers stored in the chat carry the isolation. (3) Bug fixed on the way: `BD` is a global regex — `bd()` now resets `BD.lastIndex` before `matchAll`, and `bidiPass` uses its own copies, otherwise the first characters of a run could be left outside it ("En|glish"). Measured before / after on the Director: "/pricing" drew its "/" right of the word and "0.45%" its "%" left of the number; after, both are in order. Known: answers asked in Arabic keep their Arabic HTML (with `<bdi>`) when the interface is switched to English — expected, they stay in the language they were asked in.
- Task 7.1 — live Meta connection (Facebook Pages + Instagram professional accounts, read-only), owner's request. **Server** `server/meta.js` (`createMeta(db, { config, fetch, syncLimit, now })`, mounted in `server/app.js`): config from `META_APP_ID`, `META_APP_SECRET`, `META_REDIRECT_URI`, `TOKEN_KEY` (64 hex), optional `META_GRAPH_VERSION` (default v27.0) — without all four `configured: false`. Tables (created by `createMeta`): `meta_states` (hashed one-time state, user, company, 10-minute expiry) and `meta_connections` (per company: user token and Page tokens AES-256-GCM encrypted, expiry, Pages with linked Instagram id, last sync, `reauth` flag; cascade with the company and user). Routes: `GET /api/integrations/status` → `{ meta: { configured } }`; `POST /api/companies/:id/integrations/meta/start` → `{ url }` of `https://www.facebook.com/v27.0/dialog/oauth` (scopes `pages_show_list, pages_read_engagement, pages_read_user_content, read_insights, instagram_basic, instagram_manage_insights`); `GET /api/integrations/meta/callback` (session cookie; state must be fresh, single-use, same user and still a member) exchanges the code and then a long-lived token on the server, reads `/me/accounts` (`id,name,access_token,instagram_business_account`, max 10 Pages), stores encrypted, redirects to `/?meta=connected|no_pages|denied|invalid|failed|not_configured|signin`; `GET .../integrations/meta` → `{ configured, connected, pages: [{ id, name, instagram }], connectedAt, expiresAt, lastSyncAt, needsReconnect }` (never a token); `POST .../integrations/meta/sync { days: 1–365, default 90 }` (6 per 10 min per user) reads `/{page}/feed` + per-post insights (`post_total_media_view_unique` → reach, `post_media_view` → impressions, `post_reactions_by_type_total` + `shares.count` → engagements, `post_clicks_by_type["link clicks"]` → link_clicks) and `/{ig}/media?since=` + per-media insights (`reach`, `views` → impressions, `total_interactions` → engagements), max 250 posts per sync, 4 at a time, and **returns** `{ rows, from, to, accounts, skipped: { noInsights, errors }, capped, days }`; a metric Meta does not return is left out of the row (never 0); language from the caption's script; Graph error 190 → 409 `meta_reauth` + `needsReconnect`, throttling codes → 429 `meta_rate_limited`, others → 502 `meta_failed` (detail to the server log only); every token call carries `appsecret_proof`; `DELETE .../integrations/meta` forgets the tokens. Metric names checked against Meta's reference and deprecation pages on 2026-11-13 (`post_impressions*` were removed in 2025). Comments are not counted in engagements. **Frontend** (`public/index.html`): `metaInfo(c)` (memory only); the Meta card shows Connected / Reconnect needed / Not connected, the Pages and last sync, and "Connect Meta" → Meta's screen, "Sync last 90 days", "Disconnect" (data already synced stays); sync rows go through `importData` with origin `meta-api` ("Meta (live sync)" in the import history) and a post synced again replaces its older row (no double counting); `metaReturn()` opens Integrations after Meta's redirect with an EN / AR message and cleans the URL; when the server has no Meta app the "Connect…" explanation says exactly which settings are missing, and the Google cards say their connection is not built yet. `.env.example` documents the settings. **Not done:** no call to the real Meta has been made (no Meta app) — 7.2.
- Task 7.3 — live Google connection, GA4 + Search Console with one consent (owner's request). **Server** `server/google.js` (`createGoogle(db, { config, fetch, syncLimit, now })`, mounted in `server/app.js` next to Meta; `GET /api/integrations/status` now answers `{ meta: { configured }, google: { configured } }`): config `GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID`, `GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET`, `GOOGLE_REDIRECT_URI`, `TOKEN_KEY`. Tables `google_states` (hashed one-time state, 10 minutes) and `google_connections` (per company: refresh and access tokens AES-256-GCM encrypted, access expiry, chosen `ga_property` + name, `key_event`, `gsc_site`, last sync, `reauth` flag; cascade). Routes under `/api/companies/:id/integrations/google`: `POST /start` → `{ url }` of `https://accounts.google.com/o/oauth2/v2/auth` (scopes `analytics.readonly` + `webmasters.readonly`, `access_type=offline`, `prompt=consent`, `include_granted_scopes=true`); `GET /api/integrations/google/callback` (same state rules as Meta) exchanges the code at `https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token`, requires a refresh token (`/?google=no_refresh` otherwise) and at least one of the two scopes (`no_scope`); `GET /` info (never a token); `GET /options[?property=properties/N]` → GA4 properties (Admin API v1beta `accountSummaries`), Search Console sites (`webmasters/v3/sites`, unverified ones left out) and the property's key events (`properties/N/keyEvents`); `PUT /` `{ gaProperty, keyEvent, gscSite }` — each checked against what Google says this account can read (400 `invalid_choice`); `POST /sync { only: ga4 | gsc, days 1–365 }` (10 per 10 min) → 409 `choose_property` / `choose_site` until chosen; GA4: Data API v1beta `runReport` with dimensions `date, sessionDefaultChannelGroup, landingPage, deviceCategory`, metric `sessions`, plus — only when a key event is chosen — a second report with `eventCount` filtered on `eventName` = that event, joined as `enquiries` (none chosen → no enquiries column at all, never 0); Search Console: `searchAnalytics.query` with dimensions `date, query, page`, type `web`, rowLimit 25,000 paged; date range = the last N days up to yesterday; max 5,000 rows per sync (`capped`); the access token is refreshed with the refresh token when it has under a minute left; `invalid_grant` / 401 → 409 `google_reauth` + `needsReconnect`, 429 → `google_rate_limited`, 403 → `google_forbidden`; `DELETE /` revokes the token at `https://oauth2.googleapis.com/revoke` (best effort) and forgets it. **Frontend** (`public/index.html`): `googleInfo`, `googleOpts`, `googleBlock`, `googleBtns`; the GA4 and Search Console cards share one connection — "Connect Google", then a property select (loads its key events) with "Which key event counts as an enquiry?" (None / the property's key events) and a site select, each with "Save choice"; "Sync last 90 days" per card (disabled until chosen); "Disconnect Google"; rows imported with origin `google-api` ("Google (live sync)"), and **earlier rows of the same dataset for the synced days are replaced — also rows that came from a file — so a day is never counted twice**; `metaReturn()` now also handles `?google=…` with EN / AR messages. **Fix to 7.1** (found while designing this): the JSON import took its columns from the first row only, so Meta rows whose optional columns differ (Instagram has `format`, no `link_clicks`) could lose a field or get 0 for a missing number; `importData` now uses every key of every JSON row and leaves a missing value absent (CSV unchanged); a row missing a required column is still refused. Google docs checked 2026-11-13; a few response shapes (Data API `rows[].dimensionValues/metricValues`, the error JSON) are Google's standard ones but were not on the pages read — the real check (7.4) confirms them.

- Task UX 6.1 — UX journey pass (frontend only, `public/index.html`; block "Journey (6.1)" just above "Boot"). **(a) One-step setup:** while a company has no approved fact and no imported data (`inSetup(c)`), the Dashboard is `setupView(c)`: one form (`quick` action) with company name, website (scheme added when missing), file drop (existing `files` action) and pasted text → the site is read through the existing `scan` / `fetch-url`, the name is taken from the page title when it was empty or a default (`guessName`, a toast says so), then a progress state and a result state ("Here is what BrandPilot found": ready to approve / need your decision / open questions, four examples, "Approve N source-stated facts" = the existing `kBulk`, "Review one by one"). **(b) Automatic analysis:** `addText` calls `autoDisc(c)` — a new readable source is analysed 0.5 s later without the "Run discovery" button (animated on the Dashboard, instant with a toast elsewhere); analysed sources carry `an: 1`. **Nothing is approved automatically**; findings stay pending. **(c) Next-step strip:** `journey(c)` → `{ stages, next }` from real state only; `journeyBar` renders it at the top of every screen (six stages Sources / Knowledge / Data / Plan / Campaign / Results with ticks, plus one next step with a button; the button is hidden on the screen it points to; no strip on the setup screen). Order of next steps: add sources → analyse → approve facts → (campaign exists: finish brief → Brand QA → export → import results → improvements) → add data → "Recommended campaign: <audit title>" (`recCamp`) or open audit / new campaign. `jCamp` opens a given campaign on a given screen. **(d) Navigation by workflow:** "Data & Integrations" moved under Understand, "Export & Delivery" under Create; System holds only Trust & AI settings. **(e)** The Dashboard has an ask box (existing `ask` action → Marketing Director). **(f)** The 15-step "Demo guide" shows only for demo companies. **(g)** A new account / new company lands on the Dashboard (setup), not on Company sources. All texts EN/AR. Unchanged: every existing screen, action, approval rule and server route. Known limits: the name guess is a heuristic on the page title / host name; PDF / Office files are still not read; the "discovery finished" note on Company sources can outlive a company switch (existing behaviour).

- Task UX 6.2 — Knowledge Hub review made faster (frontend only). A pending finding shows its evidence quote and source title under the statement (`.fev` in `findCard`; the "Source and evidence" details stay). The screen opens on "To review" while anything waits (`S.ui.kf` unset), otherwise on "All". A progress line "n of m findings reviewed" with a bar (`.kprog`; open questions are not counted) says what to do next (review → publish → done). A category with two or more source-stated pending facts gets "Approve the n source-stated facts here" (`kCat`: same filter as `kBulk` plus no `noEv` — `supF`; it does not publish). Flagged items (claims, interpretations, conflicts) are never touched by either bulk button. Not done from the original 6.2 wording: conflicts side by side with both sources (the inline evidence covers the common case).
- Task UX 6.4 — Data screen (frontend only): `V.integ` is wrapped to add, above the four connector cards, one drop zone that uses the existing `files` action (the dataset is recognised by its columns; several files at once) and four tiles (GA4, Leads, Meta, Search Console) showing rows added and what each dataset unlocks in the audit. The per-dataset import buttons, templates and "Remove data" are unchanged.

- Task UX 6.5 — campaign pipeline (frontend only): `campPipe(c, view)` renders, under the page header of Campaign Studio, Brand QA and Export, the current campaign's name and four steps — Brief, Assets (count), Brand QA (approved / total), Export — each a button (`jCamp` with `id|view|tab`), ticked from real state, the current one outlined, later ones disabled until assets exist. The three views are wrapped; their own content is unchanged. "Export" is ticked from the company-level `c.exported` flag (set by any package export), so with several campaigns it can tick for a campaign that was not the one exported.
- Task UX 6.3 (reduced) — Company sources: editing a basics / socials field no longer re-renders the page (`basic` saves and updates the header name in place), so keyboard focus moves on to the next field — this closes the "focus drops to body" quirk; the cards are no longer numbered 1–4, "Run discovery" is a secondary button next to "New sources are analysed automatically." **Not done:** the page layout itself was not merged into one card.

- UX 6.2 leftover — conflicts side by side (frontend): `confOpts(c)` = the offers that state a trial length while a conflict finding is pending (the only conflict the extractor produces). The conflict card spans the row and shows each version with its quote, source title and date and a "Keep this one" button (`kKeep`: approves it, rejects the other versions, closes the conflict, marks the version unpublished). **Integrity fix:** `supF(f, c)` is now the single "source-stated, safe to bulk-approve" filter (header button, per-category button, setup screen, next-step strip) and excludes the conflicting offers, so "Approve all" can no longer approve two contradicting offers.
- UX 6.3 leftover — Company sources layout: one card "Add what you already have" with three columns (website + "Read the website", documents, pasted text); "Company details and social profiles" is a collapsible card, open while the name is a default or the objective is empty; a website typed without a scheme gets `https://`.
- Task UX 6.6 — empty states: on a company with no source, any empty state that pointed at Company sources now reads "Start setup" and opens the setup screen (`empty()`); the texts no longer tell the user to "run discovery"; the Brand QA and Audit empty texts say what unlocks them. Kept small on purpose: the next-step strip already names the unlocking action on every screen.
- Task UX 6.7 — Arabic for the server's wording (frontend maps keyed by the ids the server returns, falling back to the server text): AI Director "what is needed" list (`aiNeeds`, from the new `needItems: [{ id, text }]` that `POST .../director/ask` returns next to `needs` — **the only server change**, `server/director.js`), supporting evidence lines (`supTx`: KPI names from `KPI_AR`, audit titles from the browser audit or `SRV_AR`), and the server's detail line in Brand QA when browser and server disagree (`QA_AR`). **Not done:** a native-speaker review of the Arabic (the 6.x Arabic was written and re-read by the model only); the exported `brand-qa-report.md` keeps the server's English details; stored chat answers keep the language they were asked in.

- **Arabic convention (owner, 2026-11-14): the interface addresses the user in the singular everywhere** — imperatives ("راجع", "استورد"), pronouns ("بياناتك", "اعتمادك") and verbs ("تريد"), in buttons, headings and sentences alike. All of `public/index.html` was converted (169 words: 24 plural imperatives, the "-كم" pronoun on 54 words, 16 plural verb forms); new Arabic text must follow it. Not converted: generated campaign copy and demo source documents (they address the company's customers, and none used these forms). Verified by syntax check and the five browser runs (81 checks pass); the resulting sentences were not read by a native speaker. **After the merge with `origin/dev` (2026-11-14) the Arabic that came with it was converted too:** `public/index.html` 72 words (Google / Meta cards and messages, header badge, report card, the setup questions «تبيعونها» / «تخدمونها» missed earlier), `server/audit.js` 50 (the Arabic audit titles, reasons and actions), `server/report.js` 2, and the matching strings in `test/audit.test.js` / `test/report.test.js`. Kept plural on purpose: «كيف سمعتم عنا؟» in the `src-lowq` action — it is a form question the company asks its customers. Server Arabic now follows the convention as well.

- Task 8.1 — raw-export import, dates and numbers (frontend only, `importData` in `public/index.html`; helpers `latinDigits`, `dateParts`, `numVal`, `impDate`). **Dates:** a time part is dropped (`11/08/2026 14:30`, `2026-11-08T14:30:00`, `2026-11-09 08:00`); year-first dates may use `-`, `/` or `.`; Arabic-Indic digits are read. **Day/month order is decided once per file, never per row:** any first part above 12 → day first; otherwise any second part above 12 → month first; neither → day first **assumed**. The import record carries `dord: 'dmy' | 'mdy'` (only when the file has such dates) and `dassumed: true`; `impDate(i)` words it in the toast and in the Import history (EN/AR), and an assumed order tells the user to re-export as YYYY-MM-DD if it is wrong. A file mixing both orders is read day-first and the other rows are skipped as invalid dates. **Numbers:** thousands separators (`1,234`, `1 234`, `1,234,567`, `1,234.5`, Arabic `٬`) and Arabic-Indic digits are read; `12%` and `1,5` stay "not a number", negatives stay "negative" (same counters as 3.6). The server mirror needs no change (it receives ISO dates and numbers). **For 8.2:** the default order is the literal `'dmy'` inside `importData` (`dord` line) — pass the platform default there (Meta = month first).

- Task 8.2, LinkedIn part — native LinkedIn page export recognised (frontend only, `public/index.html`: `platformMap(rows)`, `impPlat`, `impInfo`; called at the top of `importData`). **Confirmed against the owner's real export** (`data/…LinkedIn content export….xls`, git-ignored; 2 sheets): the post-level sheet is "All posts" — one notes row, then the headers `Post title, Post link, Post type, Campaign name, Posted by, Created date, Campaign start date, Campaign end date, Audience, Impressions, Views, Offsite Views, Clicks, Click through rate (CTR), Likes, Comments, Reposts, Follows, Engagement rate, Content Type`; dates are `MM/DD/YYYY` text; "Post type" is Organic / Sponsored (not a format); "Content Type", "Views", "Follows" and "Campaign name" were empty in all 42 posts. The file is recognised when one of the first six rows holds `Post link` + `Created date` + `Impressions` (case- and space-insensitive), rows above it are skipped, and it is always filed as the Meta-schema dataset with `platform: linkedin`. Mapping: `Created date`→`date`, `Post link`→`post_id`, `Impressions`→`impressions` **and** `reach` (LinkedIn exports no reach), `Likes + Comments + Reposts`→`engagements`, `Clicks`→`link_clicks` (LinkedIn counts every click on the post, not only link clicks), `Campaign name`→`campaign`, `Content Type` lower-cased→`format` (**unconfirmed: no value seen**), `Post title`→`text` (one line, ≤ 500 chars; kept in the browser state only — the server schema gets `text` in 8.5). Ambiguous dates use month-first for this platform (`dassumed` stays true and the note says "the order this platform exports"). The import record carries `plat: 'linkedin'`; toast and Import history say what was recognised and derived (EN/AR). The daily "Metrics" sheet is refused with a message pointing to "All posts". An `.xls` / `.xlsx` dropped on any file zone now gets "Excel files are not read yet… save the sheet as CSV" instead of being registered as an unreadable document. **Not done:** the Meta Business Suite mapping (no real export yet); reading the Excel file itself (8.3 — see the decision below).
- Task 8.3 — Excel import (frontend + one vendored file). **Owner's decision 2026-11-14: use SheetJS** (the real LinkedIn export is binary `.xls`, BIFF / OLE2, which the planned unzip approach cannot read). `public/vendor/xlsx.full.min.js` = SheetJS Community Edition **0.20.3** taken from the maintainers' CDN (`cdn.sheetjs.com/xlsx-0.20.3/package/dist/`; the `xlsx` package on npm is stuck at 0.18.5 and misses security fixes — do not use it), sha256 `cc015130aa8521e7f088f88898eba949ccdcbfb38df0bd129b44b7273c3a6f41`, Apache-2.0 licence next to it (`xlsx.LICENSE.txt`); `.gitattributes` keeps the folder byte-exact. It is **not** in `package.json` and is **loaded only when an Excel file is added** (`loadXLSX`), from the app's own origin. `importExcel(c, file)` (`public/index.html`, called by the `files` action for `.xls` / `.xlsx` on the Data drop zone, the setup screen and Company sources): 5 MB cap; the first bytes must be `PK` or `D0 CF 11 E0`, otherwise "this is not an Excel file" (before the library is fetched); the workbook is opened with `cellDates` and `dateNF: 'yyyy-mm-dd'` (**set on `read`, not on `sheet_to_csv`** — otherwise real date cells come out as `10/5/26`); each sheet becomes CSV text (`rawNumbers`) and goes through `importData` unchanged, a sheet named like "All posts" first, then the others, the first sheet that imports wins; the import's file name gets " — <sheet>" when the workbook has several sheets; "already imported" stops at once; if no sheet imports, the first sheet's error is shown. Known limits: only one sheet per workbook is imported; the same data added once as Excel and once as CSV is not recognised as a duplicate (the fingerprint is the text read); the per-dataset "Import CSV / JSON" buttons do not take Excel (the drop zone does); password-protected workbooks give the generic "could not be read".

- Task 8.4 — content themes list (frontend only, `public/index.html`; the server stores a fact's category as free text, so nothing changed there). New Knowledge Hub category `themes` "Content themes" / «موضوعات المحتوى» in `CATS` (before `pillars`, heading keywords: content theme, post theme, themes, content topics + Arabic) and in `CAT_ORDER` (after `pillars`). A source with such a heading yields **pending** themes like any other fact. `postThemes(c)` = the approved `themes` facts (trimmed, de-duplicated without regard to case or spacing) **plus the built-in `campaign`** — **this is the only list a post may be tagged with (use it in 8.5 / 8.6)**. On the Knowledge Hub the section (`#k-themes`) is shown on the tabs All and Approved even with no theme: a hint (themes ≠ messaging pillars), the list "Themes posts can use", and an inline form (`kAdd` with `cat=themes`, ≤ `THEME_MAX` = 60 chars) — a theme typed there is an approved fact "provided by you"; a duplicate of an allowed theme (or of `campaign`) is refused. Rename = the card's Edit, remove = Reject (existing actions). Approval rules unchanged: nothing is approved without a click. Known limits: renaming through Edit is not checked for length or duplicates (the list de-duplicates when read); the section is not shown on a Knowledge Hub that has no finding at all (that screen only offers "add a source"); renaming or removing a theme does not touch posts already tagged with it (there is no tagging screen until 8.5).

- Task 8.5 — Tag posts (frontend + one schema word on the server). **(a) Caption:** optional `text` added to the Meta schema in `SCH` (`public/index.html`) and `SCHEMAS` (`server/data.js`; strings are cut at 500 chars there as before). **(b) Suggestions never touch `theme`:** per post the browser keeps `theme_suggested`, `theme_why`, `theme_src` (`'rules'`; `'ai'` is reserved for 8.6) and `theme_ok` (the user has decided, including "No theme"); the server mirror drops all four (not schema columns), so **the audit — browser and server — sees only confirmed themes and needed no change**. `tagged(r)` = `theme` or `theme_ok`. `suggestTheme(r, themes)`: a post with a `campaign` → `campaign`; otherwise the approved theme whose name scores best in the caption (each name word of 3+ letters that is not a stop word, matched at a word start after dropping a plural ending: +1; the whole name: +2; its hashtag: +2), with the reason in words; no caption or no match → no suggestion. `suggestThemes(c)` recomputes rule suggestions for every undecided post each time the step renders (they follow the theme list and the interface language) and leaves an `'ai'` suggestion alone while its theme is still allowed. Language is suggested from the caption (Arabic script → `ar`, else `en`) and written when the post is confirmed. **(c) Screen:** `tagView(c)` replaces the Data screen while `S.ui.tag` is set (cleared by any navigation): progress "n of m posts tagged", tabs Suggested / No suggestion / Tagged / All with counts (an emptied tab moves on to the next non-empty one), one row per post, newest first, at most `TAG_PAGE` = 100 shown: date, platform, caption excerpt, theme select (approved themes + `campaign` + "No theme"), suggestion badge with its reason and a Confirm button, language select, format select. Choosing a theme by hand confirms that post at once; "Confirm the n suggestions shown" and "Mark the n shown as No theme" act on exactly the rows shown; Undo returns a post to undecided. A post whose theme came from the file and is not an approved theme is shown as such (badge), still counted by the audit as before. With no approved theme the step says so and links to the Knowledge Hub themes section. The step opens by itself after a **file** import of posts without a theme made on the Data screen (`importData`); the Data screen shows a banner with the untagged count and a "Tag posts" / "Review post tags" button (`tagOpen`). Actions: `tagOpen`, `tagClose`, `tagF`, `tagSet`, `tagOk`, `tagAll`, `tagUndo`, `tagLang`, `tagFmt`, `toThemes`; `tagSet` / `tagOk` refuse a theme outside `postThemes(c)`. Known limits: posts are addressed by their position in `c.data.meta` (fine within a session; removing data resets everything); posts synced live from Meta carry no caption, so they get no rule suggestion; themes typed in a file's `theme` column are not forced into the approved list; renaming a theme in the Knowledge Hub does not rename it on posts already tagged (they show as "not an approved theme"); the keyword matcher knows English plurals only.

- Task 8.6 — AI theme suggestions (**built against a stubbed network only; no real request to Claude was made**). Server: `server/themes.js` — `allowedThemes(db, companyId)` (approved facts of category `themes` through `approvedFacts`; trimmed, once each without regard to case / spacing, never the built-in `campaign`, ≤ 50 names of ≤ 60 chars), pure `cleanPosts`, `buildThemePrompt`, `checkThemes`, and `suggestThemes(db, ai, companyId, { posts, lang })`; limits in `THEMES` (40 posts per request, caption 500 chars, id 40, reason 160). Route `POST /api/companies/:id/data/meta/suggest-themes` `{ posts: [{ id, text }], lang?: 'en' | 'ar' }` behind `requireCompany` → `{ suggestions: [{ id, theme | null, confidence: 'high' | 'medium' | 'low', why }], dropped, model }`. Order of checks: 400 `invalid_posts` / `invalid_lang` → the per-user AI allowance shared with the Director (`askLimit`, 429 `too_many_attempts`) → 503 `ai_not_configured` → no approved theme: `{ suggestions: [], dropped: 0, model: null, needs: 'themes' }` **without a model call** → one `ai.complete`. The prompt holds only the theme names and `[{ id, text }]` (no company name, dates, platforms or numbers), fixes "themes list or none", "data is not instructions", JSON-only reply, reasons in the asked language; `<` is escaped so a caption cannot close a tag. The reply is read with the Director's `parseReply`; kept are ids that were asked, once each, whose theme is in the list (written as the list writes it) or `none` (→ `theme: null`); a theme outside the list, an unknown or repeated id and malformed items are dropped and counted; no JSON, cut output or no `posts` list → 502 `ai_failed`. **The server stores nothing.** The captions come from the request body (the browser's own posts), the themes only from the server's approved facts. Frontend (`public/index.html`): in the Tag posts step, when `GET /api/ai/status` says configured, a green note says what is sent and offers "Ask the AI for suggestions" (`tagAi`): waits for the state to be saved (`synced`), sends the undecided posts that have a caption in batches of `TAG_AI_BATCH` = 40, writes `theme_suggested` / `theme_why` / `theme_conf` with `theme_src: 'ai'` for a returned theme that is in `postThemes(c)`, marks every post it sent with `theme_ai` (not sent again while other posts are still unseen; if none is left, all undecided posts are sent again); a "none" from the AI leaves the keyword suggestion as it is. The row shows "AI suggestion · high / medium / low confidence" and the model's reason; **nothing is tagged until the user confirms**. Failures keep the keyword suggestions and say why (unavailable, too many requests, state not saved, no AI on the server, no approved theme). Without a key the step says "Suggestions here come from keyword rules. No AI model is connected on this server." Known limits: the AI's reason keeps the language it was asked in; the "campaign" theme is never suggested by the AI (posts with a campaign get it from the rules); a post with no caption is never sent.

- Fix (2026-11-14, owner report "Missing required columns: platform, reach, engagements"): the Import button on each Data card accepted only .csv/.json/.txt, so a LinkedIn .xls could not be chosen there. It now accepts .xls/.xlsx (routed to `importExcel`), clears the input so the same file can be chosen again, and a file that fully matches another dataset (e.g. a GA4 file added on the LinkedIn card) is filed under that dataset with a message instead of failing (`importData` tags the error with `other`; `A.imp` retries without a kind).
- Fix (2026-11-14, owner report): the LinkedIn Pages card on the Data screen now has "Remove data". LinkedIn rows live in the Meta dataset, so `clearData('linkedin')` removes only Meta rows with `platform = linkedin` and import records with `plat = 'linkedin'` (native LinkedIn exports); Facebook / Instagram rows stay. A LinkedIn file imported through the generic Meta template keeps its import-history line (rows are still removed).

- **OpenAI provider (owner, 2026-11-14: "the api key is gpt"; model wanted: `gpt-6-luna`).** `server/ai.js` now has two providers behind the same `createAi()` → `{ configured, provider, model, complete }` contract: Claude through the Anthropic SDK (unchanged) and **OpenAI through its Chat Completions endpoint over plain `fetch`** (no new dependency): `POST https://api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions`, key in `Authorization: Bearer`, the system prompt as the first message, `max_completion_tokens`, `reasoning_effort` only for `low` / `medium` / `high`. Environment: `OPENAI_API_KEY`, `AI_PROVIDER` (`anthropic` | `openai`; default = the key that is set, Anthropic first; a forced provider without its key is "not configured", never a silent switch), `AI_MODEL` (defaults `claude-opus-5-5` / `gpt-6-astra`). Reply handling: `finish_reason: 'length'` → `truncated`; a `refusal` text or `content_filter` → `ai_refused`; no choice / not JSON → `ai_failed`. Errors: 401 / 403 → `ai_auth_failed`; 429 → `ai_rate_limited`, except `insufficient_quota` (no credit) → `ai_failed`; timeout → `ai_timeout`; one retry on 5xx and connection failures. The provider's text stays on `cause` for the server log. `GET /api/ai/status` is unchanged (`{ configured }`). Callers (`director.js`, `themes.js`) are unchanged. **Verified with the owner's key: `GET /v1/models` lists `gpt-6-luna`, `gpt-6-astra`, `gpt-6-sol`, `gpt-6.1-sol`, `gpt-6.1-terra`, `gpt-5.6-luna` (98 models visible) — no completion request has been made yet.** Not done: the interface does not say which provider is in use (its texts say "AI model"); the manual "export context for Claude / import Claude answer" bridge is a separate feature and still names Claude.
- **Secret handling (2026-11-14):** the owner had pasted the real OpenAI key into the tracked `.env.example`. It was moved to the git-ignored `.env` (`OPENAI_API_KEY`) and `.env.example` restored before any commit; `git log -S` confirms no key was ever committed. `.env.example` now starts with a warning. **Before every commit: `git diff --cached | grep -c "sk-"` must be 0.**

- **Task 4.5 — Phase 4 exit check with a real model: passed (2026-11-14).** Provider OpenAI, model `gpt-6-luna` (owner's choice), throwaway in-memory databases, made-up company. **Phase 4 is signed off for this provider and model; Claude has still never been called for real** (no Anthropic key). One prompt change came out of it, in `buildPrompt` (`server/director.js`), for Arabic only: plain Modern Standard Arabic, address the reader in the singular (the interface convention), quote approved wording as written — before it the model wrote plural imperatives («ركّزوا», «استخدموا»). **Real check of 8.6: passed**, no change needed to `buildThemePrompt`. Results are under "Last tests". Small fix in the same commit (owner could not find the themes): the Knowledge Hub "Content themes" section now also shows on the "To review" tab, which is the tab the screen opens on.
